Credit Risk                                                              信貸風險
Credit risk refers to the risk of loss arising when a borrower,          信 貸 風 險 指 借 款 人、交 易 對 手 或 發 行 人 未 能 履
counterparty or issuer does not meet its financial obligations to        行對我們所作出財務責任產生之損失風險。
us.

We are exposed to the risk that third parties that are indebted to       我們面對欠債第三方不履行責任之風險。
us fail to perform their obligations.

This risk may arise from a variety of business activities, including     有 關 風 險 可 能 源 自 各 種 商 業 活 動，包 括 但 不 限
but not limited to entering into swap or other derivative                於 訂 立 掉 期 或 其 它 衍 生 合 約，令 交 易 對 手 有 責
contracts under which counterparties have obligations to make            任 向 我 們 付 款；通 過 各 種 貸 款 責 任 向 客 戶 提 供
payments to us; extending credit to clients through various              信 貸；提 供 以 實 物 或 金 融 抵 押 品（ 其 價 值 有 時
lending commitments; providing short or long-term funding                未 必 足 以 完 全 涵 蓋 貸 款 還 款 額）作 擔 保 之 短 期
that is secured by physical or financial collaterals whose value         或 長 期 資 金；向 結 算 所、結 算 代 理、交 易 所、銀
may sometimes be insufficient to fully cover the loan repayment          行、證 券 公 司 及 其 它 金 融 交 易 對 手 提 供 保 證 金

to clearing houses, clearing agencies, exchanges, banks,                 及 貸 款，該 等 資 產 之 價 值 可 能 會 隨 相 關 責 任 或
securities firms and other financial counterparties; and investing       貸款之已變現或預期違約而波動。
and trading in securities and loan pools whereby the value
of these assets may fluctuate based on realised or expected

Although we regularly review our credit exposures, default risk          儘 管 我 們 定 期 審 視 信 貸 風 險，但 違 約 風 險 可 能
may arise from events or circumstances that are difficult to             源 自 難 以 發 現 或 預 見 之 事 件 或 情 況。我 們 採 用
detect or foresee. We use an internal credit assessment process          內部信貸評估程序以評估潛在借款人之信貸質

the credit limits granted to each borrower. We may suffer loss on        日未能支付本金及╱或利息，及╱或抵押品（ 例
loans receivable if the principal and/or interest is not paid on the     如 聯 交 所 上 市 公 司 股 份 或 若 干 香 港 物 業）之 市

Legal, Regulatory and Compliance Risk                                  法律、監管及合規風險

Legal, regulatory and compliance risk includes the risk of legal       法 律、監 管 及 合 規 風 險 包 括 我 們 未 能 遵 守 適 用
or regulatory sanctions, material financial loss including fines,      於旗下業務之法律、法規、規則、相關自律組織
penalties, judgments, damages and/or settlements, or loss of           標 準 及 行 為 守 則 而 可 能 招 致 法 律 或 監 管 制 裁、
reputation we may suffer as a result of our failure to comply          重 大 財 務 損 失（ 包 括 罰 款、處 分、判 決、損 害 賠
with laws, regulations, rules, related self-regulatory organisation    償及╱或和解）或聲譽受損之風險。有關風險亦
standards and codes of conduct applicable to our business              包 括 合 約 及 商 業 風 險，例 如 交 易 對 手 無 法 執 行
activities. This risk also includes contractual and commercial risk    履 約 責 任 之 風 險。於 現 今 監 管 變 動 頻 繁 之 環 境
such as the risk that a counterparty’s performance obligations will    下，我 們 亦 視 監 管 變 動 為 法 律、監 管 及 合 規 風
be unenforceable. In today’s environment of rapid and possibly         險的一部分。
transformational regulatory change, we also consider regulatory
change as a component of legal, regulatory and compliance risk.


The financial services industry is subject to extensive regulations,   金 融 服 務 業 受 廣 泛 監 管，而 有 關 監 管 現 正 經 歷
which is undergoing major changes that will impact our business.       之重大變化將影響我們旗下業務。

The Group reviews potential compliance risks, such as insider          本 集 團 定 期 審 閱 潛 在 合 規 風 險，例 如 內 幕 交 易
dealing and money laundering, on a regular basis. With the             及洗黑錢活動。在外部專業顧問支持（ 如適用）
support of external professional advisers where appropriate,           下，本 集 團 監 控 是 否 需 要 就 業 務 增 長 或 擴 充 及
the Group monitors whether and the extent to which additional          金融服務業務營運應用額外監管要求及其程度。
regulatory requirements apply as a result of the growth or
expansion of our business and operations in the financial
services business.


Similar to other major financial services firms, we are subject to     與 其 它 主 要 財 務 服 務 公 司 相 若，我 們 須 受 廣 泛
extensive regulations, which significantly control the way we do       規 例 規 限，該 等 規 例 在 很 大 程 度 上 規 範 了 我 們
business and can restrict the scope of our existing businesses         經營業務之方式並可限制我們現有之業務範圍，
and limit our ability to formulate and expand our product              亦制約我們制定及拓展產品組合及追求若干投
offerings and pursue certain investments. The Group is and will        資 之 能 力。本 集 團 現 時 及 將 來 須 繼 續 受 可 能 更
continue to be subject to a possibly more complex regulatory           複 雜 之 監 管 框 架 規 限，且 日 後 將 花 費 更 多 額 外
framework, and will incur additional costs on compliance with          成本以遵守新規定及監管合規情況。
new requirements as well as to monitor for compliance in the
future.


The above risk factors should be read in conjunction with              上述風險因素須與綜合財務報表附註28所載財
financial risk management objectives and policies as set out in        務風險管理目標及政策一併閱讀。
note 28 to the consolidated financial statements.

The Company strives to be a GREAT (i.e. Growth, Respect,               在 業 務 營 運 及 人 際 管 理 上，本 公 司 以 成 為 一
Excellence, Action, Transparency) company in all of its operations     個 GREAT（ 即 增 長 (Growth)、尊 重 (Respect)、
and dealings with people. The GREAT values are the foundation          卓 越 ( E x c e l l e n c e )、行 動 ( A c t i o n ) 及 透 明
of the Company, and provide a core commitment to achieve the           (Transparency) ）的 公 司 為 目 標。GREAT價 值 是
best the Company can for all of the stakeholders.                      本 公 司 的 基 礎，亦 是 我 們 的 核 心 承 諾，令 本 公
                                                                       司為所有持份者竭盡全力，做到最好。


The culture of the Group is that the Directors and the                 本集團的文化為本集團的董事和管理層須在適
management of the Group are required to develop its business           用法律法規以及業界和社會的一般標準和期望
and operation within the boundary of the applicable laws and           的 範 圍 內 發 展 其 業 務 和 營 運。本 集 團 須 在 健 全
regulations and the general standards and expectations of              管 治 和 誠 信 基 礎 上 運 作，禁 止 各 種 破 壞 性、腐
the business community and society. The Group is required to           敗、勾結、不道德和歧視行為。
operate based on sound governance and utmost integrity and
prohibit all kinds of damaging, corruptive, collusive, unethical and
discriminative acts.

has reviewed and considered the said mechanism to be effective        及建議方面有效。

     The Company sees diversity at the Board level as an                   本公司視董事會層面之多元化乃維持高
     important element in maintaining a high standard of                   標準之企業管治的關鍵元素。本公司致力
     corporate governance. The Company is committed to a                   於建立多元化的董事會，以便擁有多元化
     diverse Board, so directors from diverse backgrounds could            背景的董事將本公司有效地推介予不同
     present the Company effectively to various constituencies,            界別，並將不同及具啟發性的觀點帶入董
     and to bring different and inspiring perspectives into the            事會。
     boardroom.

is made with a view to balancing shareholder interests with

CHAIRPERSON AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE                                       主席及行政總裁
Under code provision C.2.1 of Part 2 of the Corporate                 根 據《企 業 管 治 守 則》第2部 分 之 守 則 條 文 第
Governance Code, the roles of chairman and chief executive            C.2.1條，主 席 與 行 政 總 裁 之 職 責 應 有 所 區 分，
should be separate and should not be performed by the same            且 不 應 由 同 一 人 擔 任。目 前，本 公 司 並 無 行 政
individual. Currently, the Company does not have a chief              總 裁。與 此 同 時，由 非 執 行 董 事 兼 本 公 司 主 席
executive. Meanwhile, Ms. Li Zhongye, Cindy, a non-executive          李 中 曄 女 士 負 責 管 理 董 事 會。執 行 委 員 會（ 由
Director and the chairperson of the Company, is responsible           兩名執行董事組成）則負責業務的日常管理。職
for the management of the Board. The Executive Committee,             責 明 確 區 分 以 確 保 權 力 制 衡，防 止 權 力 集 中 於
comprising of two executive Directors, is responsible for the         任何一位人士。
day-to-day management of the business. This clear division
of responsibilities ensures a balance of power and authority,
preventing concentration of power in any one individual.

     general meetings of the Company, forthwith proceed duly

a high level of assurance, but is not a guarantee that an audit        在 之 重 大 錯 誤 陳 述。錯 誤 陳 述 可 因 欺 詐 或 錯 誤

     risk of not detecting a material misstatement resulting               蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述或淩駕內部監控之情

1.   GENERAL INFORMATION                                                      1.    一般資料
     G-Resources Group Limited (the “Company”) is an                                國 際 資 源 集 團 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）為 一
     exempted company with limited liability incorporated in                        家在百慕達註冊成立之獲豁免有限公司，
     Bermuda and its shares are listed on The Stock Exchange                        其 股 份 在 香 港 聯 合 交 易 所 有 限 公 司（「聯
     of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”). The                               交所」）上市。本公司註冊辦事處及主要營
     addresses of the registered office and principal place of                      業地點之地址已於本年報之公司資料內
     business of the Company are disclosed in the Corporate                         披露。
     Information of the annual report.


     The Company acts as an investment holding company. The                         本公司為一家投資控股公司。其主要附屬
     principal activities of its principal subsidiaries are set out in              公司之主要業務載於附註34。
     note 34.


     The consolidated financial statements are presented in                         綜 合 財 務 報 表 以 美 元（「美 元」）呈 列，有
     United States Dollars (“USD”), which is different from the                     別 於 本 公 司 之 功 能 貨 幣 港 元（「港 元」）。
     Company’s functional currency of Hong Kong Dollars                             管理層根據美元控制及監察本集團的表
     (“HKD”). The management adopted USD as presentation                            現及財政狀況，故此管理層以美元作為呈
     currency as the management controls and monitors the                           列貨幣。本集團各實體各自釐定其功能貨
     performance and financial position of the Group based on                       幣，而包括在各實體之財務報表之項目均
     USD. Each entity in the Group determines its own functional                    以該功能貨幣計量。
     currency and items included in the financial statements of
     each entity are measured using that functional currency.

     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared                 本綜合財務報表乃遵照香港會計師公會
     in accordance with HKFRS Accounting Standards issued                     頒佈之香港財務報告準則會計準則編製。
     by the HKICPA. For the purpose of preparation of the                     就編製綜合財務報表而言，倘資料合理預
     consolidated financial statements, information is considered             期會影響主要使用者的決定，則有關資料
     material if such information is reasonably expected to                   會 視 為 重 大。此 外，本 綜 合 財 務 報 表 亦 包
     influence decisions made by primary users. In addition,                  括 香 港 聯 交 所 證 券 上 市 規 則（「《上 市 規
     the consolidated financial statements include applicable                 則》」）及 香 港 公 司 條 例 規 定 之 適 用 披 露
     disclosures required by the Rules Governing the Listing of               資料。
     Securities on the Hong Kong Stock Exchange (the “Listing
     Rules”) and by the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.


     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared                 本綜合財務報表是按歷史成本基準編製
     on a historical cost basis except for certain properties and             而成，惟若干物業及金融工具於各報告期
     financial instruments that are measured at fair values at the            末以公平值計量。歷史成本一般根據貨品
     end of each reporting period. Historical cost is generally               及服務交換所得代價之公平值而釐定。
     based on the fair value of the consideration given in
     exchange for goods and services.


     Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an                公平值是於計量日期在市場參與者之間
     asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction          在有秩序交易中出售資產可收取或轉讓
     between market participants at the measurement date,                     負債須支付之價格，而不論該價格是否可
     regardless of whether that price is directly observable or               直接觀察或可使用其它估值技術估計。於
     estimated using another valuation technique. In estimating               估計資產或負責的公平值時，本集團會考
     the fair value of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into          慮該等市場參與者於計量日期對資產或
     account the characteristics of the asset or liability if market          負債定價時所考慮的資產或負債之特點。
     participants would take those characteristics into account               此等綜合財務報表中作計量及╱或披露用
     when pricing the asset or liability at the measurement date.             途之公平值乃按此基準釐定，惟不包括香
     Fair value for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in                 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第2號「以 股 份 為 基 礎 之
     these consolidated financial statements is determined on                 付 款」範 圍 內 以 股 份 為 基 礎 的 付 款 交 易、
     such a basis, except for share-based payment transactions                根 據 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第16號「租 賃」入
     that are within the scope of HKFRS 2 Share-based                         賬之租賃交易。

      A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into             非金融資產之公平值計量是參考市場參
      account a market participant’s ability to generate economic              與者可從使用該資產得到最高及最佳效
      benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by            用或將其售予另一可從使用該資產得到
      selling it to another market participant that would use the              最高及最佳效用的市場參與者所產生的
      asset in its highest and best use.                                       經濟利益。


      For financial instruments and investment properties which                就按公平值交易之金融工具及投資物業
      are transacted at fair value and a valuation technique that              以及其後期間計量公平值時使用不可觀
      unobservable inputs are to be used to measure fair value in              察輸入數據的估值技術而言，估值技術會
      subsequent periods, the valuation technique is calibrated                作出調整，使初始確認時估值技術所得的
      so that at initial recognition, the result of the valuation              結果與交易價格等同。
      technique equals the transaction price.


      In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value                此 外，就 財 務 報 告 而 言，公 平 值 計 量 根 據
      measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3                        公平值計量的輸入數據可觀察程度及對
      based on the degree to which the inputs to the fair value                其公平值計量整體的輸入數據之重要性
      measurements are observable and the significance of the                  分 類 為 第 一 級、第 二 級 及 第 三 級，詳 情 如
      inputs to the fair value measurement in its entirety, which              下：
      are described as follows:


      •     Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active            •   第一級輸入數據是實體於計量日期
            markets for identical assets or liabilities that the entity            可以在相同資產或負債取得活躍市
            can access at the measurement date;                                    場的報價（ 未經調整）；


      •     Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices                •   第二級輸入數據是除第一級計入的
            included within Level 1, that are observable for                       報 價 以 外，可 透 過 可 觀 察 市 場 數 據
            the asset or liability, either directly or indirectly, for             於資產整個年期絕大部分時間直接
            substantially the full term of the assets through                      或間接地觀察資產或負債所得出之
            corroboration with observable market data.                             輸入數據。可觀察輸入數據（ 包括同
            Observable inputs generally used to measure the fair                   類 資 產 在 活 躍 市 場 的 報 價，相 同 或
            value of financial assets classified as Level 2 include                同類資產在非活躍市場的報價及其
            quoted market prices for similar assets in active                      它可觀察市場輸入數據）一般用於計
            markets; quoted market prices in markets that are not                  量分類為第二級的金融資產之公平
            active for identical or similar assets and other market                值；及
            observable inputs; and


      •     Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset               •   第三級輸入數據是不可觀察資產或
            or liability.                                                          負債之輸入數據。

4.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICY                                         4.    主要會計政策資料（ 續）
     INFORMATION (Continued)
     Business combinations                                                    業務合併
     Acquisitions of businesses are accounted for using the                   收購業務是採用收購法入賬。業務合併的
     acquisition method. The consideration transferred in a                   轉讓代價是按公平值計量，而計算方法為
     business combination is measured at fair value, which is                 本集團所轉讓的資產、本集團向被收購方
     calculated as the sum of the acquisition-date fair values                前擁有人產生之負債及本集團於交換被
     of the assets transferred by the Group, liabilities incurred             收購方的控制權所發行的股權於收購日
     by the Group to the former owners of the acquiree and                    期之公平值總額。有關收購之費用通常於
     the equity interests issued by the Group in exchange for                 產生時於損益中確認。
     the control of the acquiree. Acquisition-related costs are
     generally recognised in profit or loss as incurred.


     Goodwill is measured as the excess of the sum of the                     商譽是以所轉讓之代價、任何非控股權益
     consideration transferred, the amount of any non-controlling             於被收購方中所佔金額及收購方以往持
     interests in the acquiree, and the fair value of the acquirer’s          有 之 被 收 購 方 股 權 之 公 平 值（ 如 有）的 總
     previously held equity interest in the acquiree (if any) over            和，減於收購日期所收購之可識別資產及
     the net amount of the identifiable assets acquired and the               所承擔之負債之淨值後所超出之差額計
     liabilities assumed as at the acquisition date. If, after re-            值。倘 經 過 重 新 評 估 後，所 收 購 之 可 識 別
     assessment, the net amount of the identifiable assets                    資產與所承擔負債之淨值高於轉讓之代
     acquired and liabilities assumed exceeds the sum of the                  價、任何非控股權益於被收購方中所佔金
     consideration transferred, the amount of any non-controlling             額及收購方以往持有之被收購方股權之
     interests in the acquiree and the fair value of the acquirer’s           公 平 值（ 如 有 ）的 總 和，該 差 額 即 時 於 損
     previously held interest in the acquiree (if any), the excess            益內確認為議價收購收益。
     is recognised immediately in profit or loss as a bargain
     purchase gain.


     Goodwill                                                                 商譽
     Goodwill arising on an acquisition of a business is carried              收購業務所產生的商譽是按業務收購日
     at cost as established at the date of acquisition of the                 期 確 立 的 成 本（ 如 上 文 會 計 政 策 所 述）減
     business, which is described in the accounting policy                    累計減值虧損（ 如有）而計算。
     above, less accumulated impairment losses, if any.


     For the purpose of impairment testing, goodwill is allocated             就減值測試而言，商譽分配至本集團各個
     to each of the Group’s cash-generating units (or groups                  現 金 產 生 的 單 位（ 或 現 金 產 生 的 單 位 組
     of cash-generating units) that is expected to benefit from               別），該 等 現 金 產 生 的 單 位 預 計 將 受 惠 於
     the synergies of the combination, which represent the                    合併產生之協同效益，即代表內部管理目
     lowest level at which the goodwill is monitored for internal             的監控商譽之最低水平，以及不大於一個
     management purpose and not larger than an operating                      經營分部。
     segment.

      asset in the unit (or group of cash-generating units). Any            於損益內確認。就商譽確認之減值虧損將

     Leases                                                                   租賃
     The Group as a lessee                                                    本集團作為承租人

     Short-term leases and leases of low-value assets                         短期租賃及低價值資產租賃

     The Group applies the short-term lease recognition                       對於自開始日期起計租期為十二個月或
     exemption to leases of land and buildings that have a lease              以內且不包括購買選擇權之土地及樓宇
     term of 12 months or less from the commencement date                     租 賃，本 集 團 可 豁 免 確 認 此 短 期 租 賃。低
     and do not contain a purchase option. It also applies the                價值資產的租賃亦可豁免確認。短期租賃
     recognition exemption for leases of low-value assets. Lease              及低價值資產租賃的租賃付款在租賃期
     payments on short-term leases and leases of low-value                    內按直線法確認為開支。
     assets are recognised as expense on a straight-line basis
     over the lease term.


     The Group as a lessor                                                    本集團作為出租人

     Classification and measurement of leases                                 租賃的分類及計量

     Leases for which the Group is a lessor are classified                    本集團作為出租人的租賃分類為融資或
     as finance or operating leases. Whenever the terms of                    經營租賃。倘租賃條款中將資產相關所有
     the lease transfer substantially all the risks and rewards               權絕大風險及回報轉移予承租人，合約則
     incidental to ownership of an underlying asset to the lessee,            分類為融資租賃。其餘租賃一概歸類為經
     the contract is classified as a finance lease. All other leases          營租賃。
     are classified as operating leases.


     Rental income from operating lease is recognised in profit               來自經營租賃的租金收入於相關租賃年
     or loss on a straight-line basis over the term of the relevant           期中按直線法於損益內確認。
     lease.


     Rental income which is derived from the Group’s ordinary                 來自本集團日常業務過程的租金收入呈
     course of business is presented as revenue.                              列為收益。


     Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                  分配代價至合約的組成部分

     The Group applies HKFRS 15 Revenue from Contracts                        本集團應用香港財務報告準則第15號「來
     with Customers (“HKFRS 15”) to allocate consideration in                 自 客 戶 合 約 之 收 益」（「香 港 財 務 報 告 準
     a contract to lease and non-lease components. Non-lease                  則 第15號」）分 配 合 約 代 價 予 租 賃 及 非 租
     components are separated from lease component on the                     賃部分。非租賃部分則按其相對獨立售價
     basis of their relative stand-alone selling prices.                      從租賃部分中區分出來。


     Refundable rental deposits                                               可退回租約按金

     HKFRS 9 and initially measured at fair value. Adjustments to             報告準則第9號入賬並按公平值初始計量。
     fair value at initial recognition are considered as additional           於初始確認時的公平值調整被視為承租
     lease payments from lessees.                                             人的額外租賃付款。

      year. Exchange differences arising, if any, are recognised             歸屬於非控股權益（ 如適用）。
      in OCI and accumulated in equity (the exchange reserve),
      attributed to non-controlling interests as appropriate.


      Goodwill and fair value adjustments on identifiable assets             收購海外業務所產生可識別資產之商譽
      acquired arising on an acquisition of a foreign operation              及公平值調整被視為該海外業務之資產
      are treated as assets and liabilities of that foreign operation        與 負 債，並 按 各 報 告 期 末 之 匯 率 換 算，產
      and translated at the rate of exchange prevailing at the               生之匯兌差額於其它全面收入內確認。於
      end of each reporting period. Exchange differences arising             出售海外業務時，有關該業務之匯兌儲備
      are recognised in OCI. Upon the disposal of a foreign                  將會獲重新分類至損益。
      operation, the exchange reserve will be subsequently
      reclassified to profit or loss.


      Retirement benefits scheme                                             退休福利計劃
      The retirement benefits scheme contributions relating to               於損益中扣除之退休福利計劃供款，指本
      the Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme for all employees                  集團按照為所有香港僱員參與之強制性
      in Hong Kong charged to profit and loss represent                      公積金計劃之規則所訂明之比率，所應付
      contributions payable to the schemes by the Group at rates             予計劃之供款。
      specified in the rules of the schemes.


      Taxation                                                               稅項
      Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently             所得稅開支是指現時應付稅項及遞延稅
      payable and deferred tax.                                              項之總和。


      The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for               現時應付稅項乃按本年度應課稅利潤計
      the year. Taxable profit differs from ‘profit before taxation’         算。應 課 稅 利 潤 與 綜 合 損 益 報 表 所 報「稅
      as reported in the consolidated statement of profit or loss            前 利 潤」不 同，乃 由 於 前 者 不 包 括 在 其 它
      because it excludes items of income or expense that are                年度應課稅或可扣稅之收益或開支以及
      taxable or deductible in other years and items that are                無需課稅或不能扣稅之項目。本集團本期
      never taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current         應付稅項按報告期末時已實施或實質上
      tax is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or            已實施之稅率計算。
      substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.

     Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                       遞延稅項是就綜合財務報表內資產及負
     between the carrying amount of assets and liabilities in the              債賬面值與計算應課稅利潤所用相應稅
     consolidated financial statements and the corresponding                   基之暫時差額予以確認。遞延稅項負債通
     tax bases used in the computation of taxable profit.                      常會就所有應課稅臨時差額確認，而遞延
     Deferred tax liabilities are generally recognised for all                 稅項資產乃按所有可能出現的可利用臨
     taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax assets are                    時差額扣稅之應課稅利潤時提撥。若於一
     generally recognised for all deductible temporary difference              項不影響應課稅利潤或會計利潤的交易
     to the extent that it is probable that taxable profits will be            中（ 除 業 務 合 併 以 外）因 首 次 確 認 資 產 及
     available against which deductible temporary differences                  負債而引致之暫時差額，該遞延稅項資產
     can be utilised. Such deferred tax assets and liabilities are             及 負 債 則 不 會 確 認。此 外，倘 暫 時 差 額 是
     not recognised if the temporary difference arises from the                來自初次確認的商譽，則該遞延稅項負債
     initial recognition (other than in a business combination) of             不予確認。
     assets and liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the
     taxable profit nor the accounting profit. In addition, deferred
     tax liabilities are not recognised if the temporary difference
     arises from the initial recognition of goodwill.


     Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary             遞延稅項負債乃就投資於附屬公司及聯
     differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and               營公司相關之應課稅暫時差額確認，惟本
     associates, except where the Group is able to control the                 集團能控制暫時差額撥回及暫時差額於
     reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that              可預見將來不會撥回之情況除外。與該等
     the temporary difference will not reverse in the foreseeable              投資及利息相關之可扣稅暫時差額所產
     future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible                       生之遞延稅項資產，僅於可能有足夠應課
     temporary differences associated with such investments                    稅利潤去應用暫時差額之利益，且預計於
     and interests are only recognised to the extent that it is                可預見將來可以撥回時確認。
     probable that there will be sufficient taxable profits against
     which to utilise the benefits of the temporary differences
     and they are expected to reverse in the foreseeable future.


     Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax               遞延稅項資產及負債乃按清償負債或變
     rates that are expected to be applicable in the period in                 現資產之期間內所預期使用之稅率，並根
     which the liability is settled or the asset is realised, based            據報告期末已實施或實際實施之稅率（ 及
     on tax rates (and tax laws) that have been enacted or                     稅法）去計算。
     substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.


     The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets                    遞延稅項負債及資產之計量反映按照本
     reflects the tax consequences that would follow from the                  集團預期於報告期末可收回或結算其資
     manner in which the Group expects, at the end of the                      產及負債之賬面值方式計算而得出之稅
     reporting period, to recover or settle the carrying amount of             務結果。
     its assets and liabilities.

      recovered entirely through sale, unless the presumption is              別 論。倘 投 資 物 業 為 可 予 折 舊，且 持 有 之

      Financial instruments                                                   金融工具
      Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised in the        當一家集團實體成為有關工具該合約條
      consolidated statement of financial position when a group               文之訂約方時，金融資產及金融負債會於
      entity becomes a party to the contractual provisions of the             綜合財務狀況表確認。所有金融資產之日
      instruments. All regular way purchases or sales of financial            常 買 賣，於 交 易 日 確 認 及 終 止 確 認。日 常
      assets are recognised and derecognised on a trade                       買賣乃指購買或出售根據有關市場規則
      date basis. Regular way purchases or sales of financial                 或慣例設定之時限內交付之金融資產。
      assets that require delivery of assets within the time frame
      established by regulation or convention in the marketplace.


      The effective interest method is a method of calculating the            實際利率法是一種計算金融資產或金融
      amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability and          負債之已攤銷成本以及將利息收入分配
      of allocating interest income and interest expense over the             予有關期間之方法。實際利率是將估計未
      relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate that           來現金收入（ 包括所有構成實際利率整體
      exactly discounts the estimated future cash receipts and                部分之已付或已收費用、交易成本及其它
      payments (including all fees paid or received that form an              溢 價 或 折 扣）透 過 債 務 工 具 之 預 期 可 使 用
      integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction costs         年 期 或（ 倘 適 用）更 短 期 間 準 確 地 貼 現 至
      and other premiums or discounts) through the expected                   於初步確認時之賬面淨值之利率。
      life of the financial asset and financial liability, or, where
      appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount
      on initial recognition.


      Interest income from a financial asset or margin financing;             來自一個金融資產或保證金融資之利息
      and distribution and dividend income from financial                     收入；及來自本集團日常業務過程的金融
      products which are derived from the Group’s ordinary                    產品所產生的分派收入及股息收入呈列
      course of business are presented as revenue.                            為收益。

     Impairment of financial assets under expected credit loss                 預 期 信 貸 虧 損（「預 期 信 貸 虧 損」）模 型 下
     (“ECL”) model                                                             之金融資產減值

     The Group recognises a loss allowance for ECL on financial                本 集 團 針 對 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第9號 範 圍
     assets which are subject to impairment under HKFRS 9                      內須進行減值的金融資產（ 包括應收及其
     (including accounts and other receivables, loans receivable,              它 應 收 賬 款、應 收 貸 款、按 攤 銷 成 本 計 量
     investments in debt instruments measured at amortised                     之債務工具投資、銀行信託賬戶結餘以及
     cost, bank trust accounts balances and cash and cash                      現 金 及 現 金 等 值 項 目）的 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 確
     equivalents). The amount of ECL is updated at each                        認虧損撥備。預期信貸虧損的金額於每個
     reporting date to reflect changes in credit risk since initial            報告日期予以更新，以反映自初始確認以
     recognition.                                                              來信貸風險的變動。


     Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from                     全期預期信貸虧損指於相關工具預期壽
     all possible default events over the expected life of the                 命內發生所有可能的違約事件而導致的
     relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”)                預 期 信 貸 虧 損。相 反，十 二 個 月 預 期 信 貸
     represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is expected                   虧（「十 二 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損」）損 則 指 預
     to result from default events that are possible within 12                 期於報告日期後十二個月內可能發生的
     months after the reporting date. Assessments are done                     違約事件而導致的部分全期預期信貸虧
     based on the Group’s historical credit loss experience,                   損。預期信貸虧損根據本集團過往信貸虧
     adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors,                    損經驗進行評估，並根據債務人特定因素、
     general economic conditions and an assessment of both                     一般經濟狀況及於報告日期對當前狀況
     the current conditions at the reporting date as well as the               及未來狀況預測的評估而作出調整。
     forecast of future conditions.

      (ii)    Definition of default                                               (ii)    違約之定義

              For internal credit risk management, the Group                               就 內 部 信 貸 風 險 管 理 而 言，本 集 團
              considers an event of default occurs when information                        認 為，如 內 部 產 生 或 外 部 來 源 資 料
              developed inter nally or obtained from exter nal                             顯 示，債 務 人 很 可 能 無 法 向 債 權 人
              sources indicates that the debtor is unlikely to pay its                    （ 包 括 本 集 團）悉 數 還 款（ 不 計 及 本
              creditors, including the Group, in full (without taking                      集團所持有任何抵押品），則發生違
              into account any collaterals held by the Group).                             約事件。


              Irrespective of the above, the Group considers                              不論以上所述，本集團認為，倘金融
              that default has occurred when a financial asset                            資 產 逾 期 超 過 九 十 天，則 違 約 已 經
              is more than 90 days past due unless the Group                              發 生，惟 本 集 團 擁 有 合 理 及 有 依 據
              has reasonable and supportable information to                               資料顯示一項更滯後的違約標準更
              demonstrate that a more lagging default criterion is                        為合適，則另作別論。
              more appropriate.


      (iii)   Credit-impaired financial assets                                    (iii)   受信貸減值財務資產

              A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more                       如 一 項 或 多 項 違 約 事 件 發 生，導 致
              events of default that have a detrimental impact on                         金融資產的估計未來現金流量受到
              the estimated future cash flows of that financial asset                     負 面 影 響，則 該 金 融 資 產 屬 受 信 貸
              have occurred. Evidence that a financial asset is                           減 值。金 融 資 產 受 信 貸 減 值 之 證 據
              credit-impaired includes observable data about the                          包括有關以下事件之可觀察數據：
              following events:


              •      significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the                •   發行人或借款人出現嚴重財務
                     borrower;                                                                困難；


              •      a breach of contract, such as a default or past                      •   違約，例如逾期或拖欠事件；
                     due event;


              •      the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or                       •   借款人之貸款人因與借款人出
                     contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                           現財務困難相關經濟或合約理
                     financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower                     由授予借款人貸款人一般不會
                     a concession(s) that the lender(s) would not                             考慮的寬免；或
                     otherwise consider; or


              •      it is becoming probable that the borrower                            •   借款人很可能將宣佈破產或進
                     will enter bankruptcy or other financial                                 行財務重組。
                     reorganisation.

     (iv)   Write-off policy                                                              (iv)    撇銷政策

            The Group writes off a financial asset when there                                     如有資料顯示對手方出現嚴重財務
            is information indicating that the counterparty is in                                 困難且並無實際收回可能性（ 例如，
            severe financial difficulty and there is no realistic                                 對手方被清盤或已進入破產程序），
            p ro s p e c t o f re c o v e r y, f o r e x a m p l e , w h e n t h e                則 本 集 團 將 有 關 金 融 資 產 撇 銷。已
            counterparty has been placed under liquidation or                                     撇銷金融資產仍可面臨本集團收回
            has entered into bankruptcy proceedings. Financial                                    款 項 程 序 之 強 制 執 行 活 動，包 括 尋
            assets written off may still be subject to enforcement                                求 法 律 意 見（ 如 適 用）。任 何 收 回 款
            activities under the Group’s recovery procedures,                                     項確認於損益。
            taking into account legal advice where appropriate.
            A write-off constitutes a derecognition event. Any
            subsequent recoveries are recognised in profit or loss.


     (v)    Measurement and recognition of ECL                                            (v)     預期信貸虧損的計量及確認

            The measurement of ECL is a function of the                                           預 期 信 貸 虧 損 的 計 量 為 違 約 概 率、
            probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the                                  違 約 損 失 率 程 度（ 即 倘 發 生 違 約 時
            magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and                                      的 損 失 程 度 ）及 違 約 風 險 敞 口 的 函
            the exposure at default. The assessment of the                                        數。違 約 概 率 及 違 約 損 失 率 程 度 之
            probability of default and loss given default is based                                評 估 乃 按 過 往 數 據 為 依 據，並 按 前
            on historical data adjusted by forward-looking                                        瞻 性 資 料 作 出 調 整。預 期 信 貸 虧 損
            information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased                                   的 預 估 乃 無 偏 概 率 加 權 平 均 金 額，
            and probability-weighted amount that is determined                                    以發生違約的風險為權重確定。
            with the respective risks of default occurring as the
            weights.


            Generally, the ECL is estimated as the difference                                     一 般 而 言，預 期 信 貸 虧 損 以 根 據 合
            between all contractual cash flows that are due to                                    約中應付本集團的所有合約現金流
            the Group in accordance with the contract and all                                     量與本集團預期收取的所有現金流
            the cash flows that the Group expects to receive,                                     量 之 間 的 差 額 進 行 估 算，並 按 初 始
            discounted at the effective interest rate determined at                               確認時釐定的實際利率折現。
            initial recognition.

     Financial liabilities and equity instruments                                           金融負債及股本工具

     Classification on debt or equity instruments                                           債務或股本工具之分類

     Debt and equity instruments issued by a group entity are                               集團實體所發行之債務及股本工具乃根
     classified as either financial liabilities or as equity according                      據所訂立合約安排之內容及金融負債和
     to the substance of the contractual arrangements entered                               股本工具之定義分類為金融負債或股本。
     into and the definitions of a financial liability and an equity
     instrument.


     Interest expense is recognised on an effective interest                                利息開支按實際利率法確認。
     basis.


     Financial liabilities                                                                  金融負債

     All financial liabilities are subsequently measured at                                 所有金融負債其後採用實際利率法已按
     amortised cost, using the effective interest method.                                   攤銷成本計量。


     Equity instruments                                                                     股本工具

     An equity instrument is any contract that evidences a                                  股本工具是任何合約證明當實體之資產
     residual interest in the assets of an entity after deducting all                       扣除全部負債後剩餘之權益。本公司所發
     of its liabilities. Equity instruments issued by the Company                           行股本工具，按已收所得款項扣除直接發
     are recorded at the proceeds received, net of direct issue                             行成本後列賬。
     costs.


     Derecognition                                                                          取消確認

     F i n a n c i a l a s s e t s a re d e re c o g n i s e d o n l y w h e n t h e        僅從資產收取現金流量之合約權利已屆
     contractual rights to receive cash flows from the assets                               滿，或當其金融資產及其金融資產擁有權
     expire, or when it transfers the financial assets and                                  之絕大部分風險及回報轉讓予另一實體
     substantially all the risks and rewards of ownership of                                時，該 金 融 資 產 即 取 消 確 認。倘 本 集 團 既
     the financial assets to another entity. If the Group neither                           不轉讓亦不保留金融資產擁有權之絕大
     transfers nor retains substantially all the risks and rewards                          部分風險及回報，且繼續控制獲轉讓資產，
     of ownership and continues to control the transferred                                  則本集團確認其於有關資產的保留權益，
     asset, the Group recognises its retained interest in the                               以及就其可能需要支付款項的相關負債。
     asset and an associated liability for amounts it may have                              倘本集團保留金融資產擁有權之絕大部
     to pay. If the Group retains substantially all the risks and                           分風險及回報，則本集團繼續確認有關資
     rewards of ownership of a transferred financial asset, the                             產，且亦就所得款項確認一項有抵押借款。
     Group continues to recognise the financial asset and also
     recognises a collateralised borrowing for the proceeds
     received.


     On derecognition of a financial asset measured at amortised                            取消確認按攤銷成本計量之金融資產時，
     cost, the difference between the asset’s carrying amount                               資產賬面值與已收代價總和之差額，於損
     and the sum of the consideration received and receivables                              益確認。
     is recognised in profit or loss.

     which the corporate asset belongs, and is compared with

     estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present            價值時，估計未來現金流量乃使用除稅前

     risks specific to the asset (or a cash-generating unit) for            位）特 有 之 風 險（ 未 來 現 金 流 量 之 估 計 並
     which the estimates of future cash flows have not been                 未就此調整）。
     adjusted.

     unit) is estimated to be less than its carrying amount, the            金 額 少 於 其 賬 面 值，則 該 項 資 產（ 或 現 金

     is reduced to its recoverable amount. In allocating the                於分配減值虧損時，減值虧損首先分配以

     the carrying amount of each asset in the unit. The carrying            至低於其公平值減出售成本（ 倘可計量）、
     amount of an asset is not reduced below the highest of                 其 使 用 價 值（ 倘 可 釐 定 ）及 零（ 以 最 高 者
     its fair value less costs of disposal (if measurable), its             為 準）。分 配 至 資 產 之 減 值 虧 損 金 額 則 按
     value in use (if determinable) and zero. The amount of the             比例分配至單位之其它資產。減值虧損即

     Company are required to make judgments, estimates and                   易見的資產及負債賬面值作出判斷、估計
     assumptions about the carrying amounts of assets and                    及假設。該等估計及相關假設乃以過往經
     liabilities that are not readily apparent from other sources.           驗及被視為有關之其它因素為基礎。實際
     The estimates and underlying assumptions are based on                   結果或會有別於該等估計。
     historical experience and other factors that are considered
     to be relevant. Actual results may differ from these
     estimates.


     The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on                有關估計及相關假設須持續予以檢討。倘
     an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are                 會計估計之修訂僅影響作出修訂之期間，
     recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised               則有關修訂會在該期間確認，而倘修訂對

     current and future periods.

     Deferred taxation on investment properties                              投資物業之遞延稅項

     For the purposes of measuring deferred tax arising from                 就計量採用公平值模式計量之投資物業
     investment properties that are measured using the fair                  所產生的遞延稅項而言，本公司董事經審
     value model, the directors of the Company have reviewed                 查本集團投資物業組合，並認為本集團投
     the Group’s investment property portfolios and concluded                資物業並非以目的為按時間消耗該等投
     that the Group’s investment properties are not held under a             資物業內篏之絕大部分的經濟利益之商
     business model whose objective is to consume substantially              業 模 式 持 有。因 此，於 計 量 本 集 團 投 資 物
     all of the economic benefits embodied in the investment                 業之遞延稅項時，本公司董事已決定不推
     properties over time. Therefore, in measuring the Group’s               翻採用公平值模式計量之投資物業賬面
     deferred taxation on investment properties, the directors of            值透過銷售全部收回之假設。故本集團並
     the Company have determined that the presumption that                   無就投資物業公平值變動確認任何遞延
     the carrying amounts of investment properties measured                  稅項，原因為本集團出售投資物業時毋須
     using the fair value model are recovered entirely through               就公平值變動繳納任何所得稅。
     sale is not rebutted. As a result, the Group has not
     recognised any deferred taxes on changes in fair value of
     investment properties as the Group is not subject to any
     income taxes on the fair value changes of the investment
     properties on disposal.

      Estimated impairment of goodwill                                       商譽之估計減值

      Determining whether goodwill is impaired requires an                   釐訂商譽是否已減值需對已獲分配商譽
      estimation of the recoverable amount of the cash-                      之現金產生單位的可收回金額進行估計，
      generating unit to which goodwill has been allocated, which            即使用價值或公平值減出售成本之較高
      is the higher of the value in use or fair value less costs of          者。使用價值計算要求本集團經考慮其它
      disposal. The value-in-use calculation requires the Group to           關鍵假設（ 包括未來增長率及預期毛利率）
      estimate the future cash flows expected to arise from the              估計預期該現金產生單位將產生的未來
      cash-generating unit and a suitable discount rate in order             現 金 流 及 合 適 的 貼 現 率，以 計 算 現 值。如
      to calculate the present value, taking into account other              實際未來現金流低於預期，或如事實及情
      key assumptions including future growth rate and expected              況出現變動導致未來現金流調低，或會產
      gross margin. Where the actual future cash flows are less              生重大減值虧損。
      than expected, or change in facts and circumstances which
      results in downward revision of future cash flows, a material
      impairment loss may arise.


      As at 31 December 2025, the carrying amount of goodwill                於 二 零 二 五 年 十 二 月 三 十 一 日，商 譽 的

27. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                          27. 資本風險管理
  The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in                 本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團旗下各
  the Group will be able to continue as a going concern                    實體將可以持續經營，並透過優化債務與
  while maximising the return to shareholders through the                  權益間之平衡比例，為股東帶來最大回報。
  optimisation of the debt and equity balances. The Group’s                於過往年度，本集團之整體策略維持不變。
  overall strategy remains unchanged from the previous year.


  The capital structure of the Group consists of equity                    本集團之資本結構包括本公司擁有人應
  attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued                 佔 權 益（ 包 括 已 發 行 股 本、儲 備 及 保 留 盈
  share capital, reserves and retained earnings.                           利）。

       the financial institutions with high credit rating to be                風 險 低 的 金 融 資 產。本 集 團 管 理 層
       low credit risk financial assets. The management                        認為，由於發行人具有高信貸評級，
       of the Group considers the probability of default is                    違約機率微乎其微，因此，並無確認
       negligible on the basis of high credit rating issuers,                  任何虧損撥備。
       accordingly, no loss allowance was recognised.

31. RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEMES                                         31. 退休福利計劃
   The Group participates in a Mandatory Provident Fund                       本集團為其所有香港僱員參與根據強制
   Scheme (the “MPF Scheme”) established under the                            性公積金計劃條例設立的強積金計劃（「強
   Mandatory Provident Fund Schemes Ordinance, for all its                    積金計劃」）。強積金計劃之資產由受托人
   employees in Hong Kong. The assets of the MPF Scheme                       所管理的基金持有，其乃獨立於本集團之
   are held separately from those of the Group, in funds under                資 產。根 據 強 積 金 計 劃 規 則，僱 主 及 其 僱
   the control of trustees. Under the rules of MPF Scheme,                    員須各自按規則所訂明之比率向強積金
   the employer and its employees are each required to make                   計劃供款。本集團就強積金計劃之唯一責
   contributions to MPF Scheme at rates specified in the                      任為根據強積金計劃作出所需供款。本集
   rules. The only obligation of the Group with respect to MPF                團概無沒收供款，可供用作減少往後年度
   Scheme is to make the required contributions under the                     作出之供款。
   MPF Scheme. No forfeited contributions are available to
   reduce the contributions payable in future years.

   During the year ended 31 December 2025, the total                          截至二零二五年十二月三十一日止年度，

32. RECONCILIATION OF LIABILITIES                                       32. 融資活動所產生的負債之對賬
    ARISING FROM FINANCING ACTIVITIES
   During the year ended 31 December 2025, there were no                      截至二零二五年十二月三十一日止年度，
   liabilities arising from financing activities. Liabilities arising         概無融資活動產生的負債。融資活動所產
   from financing activities are those for which cash flows                   生的負債乃為於本集團綜合現金流量表
   were, or future cash flow will be, classified in the Group’s               內歸類為融資活動所產生的現金流之過
   consolidated statement of cash flows as cash flows from                    往現金流或未來現金流。因此，無需對賬。
   financing activities. As a result, no reconciliation is required.
